"Generator在设计Rubric时，针对此类任务应设立一条核心准则：'回复是否准确识别了原句中不自然或错误的表达（如错别字、误用的短语），并将其替换为符合语境的正确表达？'。同时，必须明确排除‘只要保留了原句中的错误词汇即视为保留原意’的逻辑，避免将‘修正错误’误判为‘篡改意图’。若原句存在公认的习语错误（如go along vs get "application_guide": "Generator在设计评分标准时，若检测到任务前提为明显荒谬且用户寻求估算/推演：1. 必须移除任何禁止提供具体数值的准则；2. 将‘是否构建了一个自洽的虚构逻辑链条并给出估算结果’设为正向得分点；3. 仅当回答完全拒绝参与推演（如仅说‘不可能’而无后续互动）时才扣分；4. Book'等）的情况？’。若发现模型直接复用原作核心设定，即使结构完整也应判定不合格；若模型使用了原创名称但逻辑相似，则判定合格。此标准应优先于‘结构完整性’检查，防止模型通过简单替换名词或直接挪用原作设定来规避‘原创’要求。", "retrieval_source": "semantic", "historical_reward": null, "evidence_tasks": 0}, Here are some examples:\n\n1. The Camp David Accords (1978): This was a peace treaty between Egypt and Israel, mediated by U.S. President Jimmy Carter. The agreement led to the normalization of relations between the two countries, and Israel's withdrawal from the Sinai Peninsula, which it had occupied since the Six-Day War in 1967. The accords were a result of intense negotiations and conflict resolution efforts.\n\n2. The Good Friday Agreement (1998): Also known as the Belfast Agreement, this was a major step in the Northern Ireland peace process. It was an agreement between the British and Irish governments and most of the political parties in Northern Ireland, aiming to end the Troubles, a thirty-year conflict. The agreement established a power-sharing government and addressed issues of sovereignty, civil rights, and decommissioning of weapons.\n\n3. The Dayton Accords (1995): This agreement ended the Bosnian War, a conflict that lasted from 1992 to 1995 and resulted in the deaths of over 100,000 people. The accords were negotiated at Wright-Patterson Air Force Base near Dayton, Ohio, under the auspices of the United States. The agreement created a federalized Bosnia and Herzegovina, with two entities: the Federation of Bosnia and Herzegovina and Republika Srpska.\n\n4. The Oslo Accords (1993): These were a set of agreements between Israel and the Palestine Liberation Organization (PLO), aiming to achieve a peace treaty.
